=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def dp(self,i,s,prev,k,ct,n,dct:
        if k<0:
            return float("infinity")
        if i>=n:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            return x
        if (i,prev,ct,k) in dct:
            return dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]
        if s[i]==prev:
            inc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k,ct+1,n,dct)
        else:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            inc=x+self.dp(i+1,s,s[i],k,1,n,dct)
        exc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k-1,ct,n,dct)
        dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]=max(inc,exc)
        return min(inc,exc)


    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        n=len(s)
        return self.dp(0,s,"",k,0,n,{})

class Solution:
    def dp(self,i,s,prev,k,ct,n,dct:
        if k<0:
            return float("infinity")
        if i>=n:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            return x
        if (i,prev,ct,k) in dct:
            return dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]
        if s[i]==prev:
            inc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k,ct+1,n,dct)
        else:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            inc=x+self.dp(i+1,s,s[i],k,1,n,dct)
        exc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k-1,ct,n,dct)
        dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]=max(inc,exc)
        return min(inc,exc)


    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

class Solution:
    def dp(self, i, s, prev, k, ct, n, dct):
        if k < 0:
            return float("inf")
        if i >= n:
            x = 0
            if ct > 1:
                x = len(str(ct)) + 1
            elif ct == 1:
                x = 1
            return x
        key = (i, prev, ct, k)
        if key in dct:
            return dct[key]
        if s[i] == prev:
            inc = self.dp(i + 1, s, prev, k, ct + 1, n, dct)
        else:
            x = 0
            if ct > 1:
                x = len(str(ct)) + 1
            elif ct == 1:
                x = 1
            inc = x + self.dp(i + 1, s, s[i], k, 1, n, dct)
        exc = self.dp(i + 1, s, prev, k - 1, ct, n, dct)
        dct[key] = min(inc, exc)
        return dct[key]

    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        return self.dp(0, s, "", k, 0, len(s), {})

def solve(i, prev, cnt, k):
    if k < 0: return inf
    if i == n: return 0
    ans = solve(i+1, prev, cnt, k-1) # delete
    if s[i] == prev:
        ans = min(ans, solve(i+1, prev, cnt+1, k))
    else:
        ans = min(ans, get_len(cnt) + solve(i+1, s[i], 1, k))
    return ans

Let's derive correct recurrence. We can define dp(i, prev, cnt, k) as minimal compressed length for suffix s[i:] given that before this suffix there is an ongoing run of character prev of length cnt. The cost of that ongoing run is not yet counted. At the end of the string, we must pay cost for the ongoing run. So base returns get_len(cnt). When we keep a different char, we pay cost for previous run and start new run. This is exactly our DP. So our DP is correct. === RAW ANSWER ===
```python
class Solution:
    def dp(self, i, s, prev, k, ct, n, dct):
        if k < 0:
            return float("inf")
        if i >= n:
            x = 0
            if ct > 1:
                x = len(str(ct)) + 1
            elif ct == 1:
                x = 1
            return x

        key = (i, prev, ct, k)
        if key in dct:
            return dct[key]

        if s[i] == prev:
            inc = self.dp(i + 1, s, prev, k, ct + 1, n, dct)
        else:
            x = 0
            if ct > 1:
                x = len(str(ct)) + 1
            elif ct == 1:
                x = 1
            inc = x + self.dp(i + 1, s, s[i], k, 1, n, dct)

        exc = self.dp(i + 1, s, prev, k - 1, ct, n, dct)

        dct[key] = min(inc, exc)
        return dct[key]

    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        return self.dp(0, s, "", k, 0, len(s), {})
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
class Solution:
    def dp(self, i, s, prev, k, ct, n, dct):
        if k < 0:
            return float("inf")
        if i >= n:
            x = 0
            if ct > 1:
                x = len(str(ct)) + 1
            elif ct == 1:
                x = 1
            return x

        key = (i, prev, ct, k)
        if key in dct:
            return dct[key]

        if s[i] == prev:
            inc = self.dp(i + 1, s, prev, k, ct + 1, n, dct)
        else:
            x = 0
            if ct > 1:
                x = len(str(ct)) + 1
            elif ct == 1:
                x = 1
            inc = x + self.dp(i + 1, s, s[i], k, 1, n, dct)

        exc = self.dp(i + 1, s, prev, k - 1, ct, n, dct)

        dct[key] = min(inc, exc)
        return dct[key]

    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        return self.dp(0, s, "", k, 0, len(s), {})
